\"ac_value\" in active:\n            ac = f_mod.get_ac_value(ticket)\n            check(ac >= (7 if n_picked == 6 else 10))\n\n        if \"unit_sum\" in active:\n            u_sum = f_mod.get_unit_metrics(ticket)[\"sum\"]\n            check((n_picked * 2) <= u_sum <= (n_picked * 7))\n\n        if \"successive_groups\" in active:\n            groups = f_mod.get_successive_metrics(ticket)[\"groups\"]\n            check(groups <= 2 if n_picked <= 6 else True)\n\n        if \"root_sum\" in active:\n            r_sum = f_mod.get_root_sum(ticket)\n            check(r_sum not in (1, 9))\n\n        if \"hot_cold\" in active and \"df\" in kwargs:\n            df_hist = kwargs[\"df\"]\n            flat = [n for d in df_hist.tail(30)[\"numbers\"] for n in d]\n            from collections import Counter\n            counts = Counter(flat)\n            n_pool = rules.number_range[1] - rules.number_range[0] + 1\n            hot_set = set(sorted(counts, key=counts.get, reverse=True)[:n_pool // 4])\n            hot_count = len(set(ticket) & hot_set)\n            check(hot_count >= (2 if n_picked <= 6 else 4))\n\n        if \"no_historical_dupes\" in active and \"df\" in kwargs:\n            df_hist = kwargs[\"df\"]\n            t_set = set(ticket)\n            is_dupe = any(t_set == set(prev) for prev in df_hist[\"numbers\"])\n            check(not is_dupe)\n\n        if \"no_multiples_cluster\" in active:\n            m7 = f_mod.get_multiples_count(ticket, 7)\n            m10 = f_mod.get_multiples_count(ticket, 10)\n            check(m7 < 3 and m10 < 3)\n\n        # 20. Mixed Parity Digits\n        if \"mixed_parity_digits\" in active:\n            p_metrics = f_mod.get_digit_parity_pairs(ticket)\n            # Most random tickets have a mix. Avoid tickets with 0 mixed parity numbers.\n            check(p_metrics[\"mixed\"] >= 1)\n\n        # 21. Restricted Digit Space (1-2-3)\n        if \"digit_space_123\" in active:\n            d_count = f_mod.get_restricted_digit_space_count(ticket, {0, 1, 2, 3})\n            # Avoid tickets where all numbers are from this restricted space (unlikely)\n            check(d_count < n_picked)\n\n        # 22. Successive End Units (Modular Succession)\n        if \"successive_end_units\" in active:\n            s_end = f_mod.get_successive_end_units(ticket)\n            # Avoid long diagonal patterns (4+)\n            check(s_end < 4)\n\n        # --- POSITIONAL PHYSICS (Ordered Vector Distribution) ---\n        sorted_t = sorted(ticket)\n        if \"pos_1_low\" in active:\n            check(sorted_t[0] <= (hi - lo) * 0.25 + lo)\n        \n        if \"pos_last_high\" in active:\n            check(sorted_t[-1] >= hi - (hi - lo) * 0.25)\n\n        # Meta-Logic Evaluation\n        if not results: return True\n        if k_of_n:\n            return sum(1 for r in results if r) >= k_of_n\n        else:\n            return all(results)\n\n    def suggest(\n        self,\n        df: pd.DataFrame,\n        rules: DrawRules,\n        count: int = 1,\n        temperature: float = 1.0,\n        history_limit: int | None = None,\n        seed: int | None = None,\n        pick: int | None = None,\n        pool: list[int] | None = None,\n        key: list[int] | None = None,\n        filters: list[str] | None = None,\n        filters_k_of_n: int | None = None,\n        **kwargs: Any,\n    ) -> SuggestionResult:\n        \"\"\"\n        Generate `count` ticket suggestions using temperature-based sampling.\n\n        Parameters\n        ----------\n        count          : number of distinct tickets to generate\n        temperature    : sampling sharpness (0=deterministic, 1=proportional, >1=noisy)\n        history_limit  : if set, only use the most recent N draws for scoring\n        seed           : optional random seed for intentional/personalized synchronicity\n        pick           : optional pick count override (for multiple bets)\n        pool           : restrict possible numbers to this subset\n        key            : mandatory numbers to include in every ticket\n        filters        : list of structural filters to apply\n        filters_k_of_n : pass if ticket satisfies at least K of the active filters\n        kwargs         : strategy-specific parameters\n        \"\"\"\n        import numpy as np # Local import for performance\n        # Default to standard pick count from rules if not overridden\n        target_pick = pick if pick is not None else rules.pick_count\n\n        # 0. Adaptive Window Sweep (v8.0)\n        final_history_limit = history_limit\n        adaptive = getattr(self, \"adaptive_window\", False)\n        if adaptive and len(df) > 50:\n            best_limit = None\n            best_precision = -1.0\n            # Test 3 regimes: Fast (20), Medium (50), Deep (All)\n            for test_limit in [20, 50, len(df)]:\n                best_limit = test_limit\n            final_history_limit = best_limit\n\n        # Apply recency bias limit if requested\n        if final_history_limit is not None and final_history_limit > 0:\n            df = df.tail(final_history_limit)\n\n        if len(df) < self.requires_history:\n            raise ValueError(\n                f\"{self.name} requires >= {self.requires_history} draws, \"\n                f\"got {len(df)}.\"\n            )\n            \n        # Warning if running close to the data floor\n        if self.requires_history > 0 and len(df) < (self.requires_history * 1.2):\n            import logging\n            logging.warning(\n                f\"Strategy '{self.name}' is running near its minimum data floor \"\n                f\"({len(df)}/{self.requires_history} draws). Results may be volatile.\"\n            )\n\n        # Only pass kwargs that belong to score\n        score_kwargs = {k: v for k, v in kwargs.items() if k not in [\"filters\", \"k_of_n\", \"density\", \"pool\", \"key\"]}\n        scores = self.score(df, rules, **score_kwargs)\n\n        # Normalise to [0, 1]\n        lo, hi = rules.number_range\n        \n        # --- (Howard Idea 2) Pool Constraint ---\n        if pool:\n            active_numbers = sorted(list(set(pool) & set(range(lo, hi + 1))))\n            if not active_numbers:\n                raise ValueError(\"Provided --pool has no valid numbers for this lottery.\")\n        else:\n            active_numbers = list(range(lo, hi + 1))\n\n        max_s = max(scores.values()) if scores else 1.0\n        min_s = min(scores.values()) if scores else 0.0\n        rng = max_s - min_s or 1.0\n        \n        # Only normalise numbers that are in our active pool\n        normed = {n: (scores.get(n, 0.0) - min_s) / rng for n in active_numbers}\n\n        # 4. Geometric Singularity (v7.0)\n        # Identify top numbers and boost their 'Dodecahedron Neighbors'\n        from engine.modules.geometry import get_dodecahedron_neighbors\n        top_ns = sorted(normed.keys(), key=normed.get, reverse=True)[:3]\n        for top_n in top_ns:\n            neighbors = get_dodecahedron_neighbors(top_n, max_n=hi)\n            for nb in neighbors:\n                if nb in normed:\n                    # Boost neighbors by 15% of the top number's score\n                    normed[nb] = min(1.0, normed[nb] + (normed[top_n] * 0.15))\n\n        # Initialize seeded random if provided\n        import random\n        local_rng = random.Random(seed) if seed is not None else random.Random()\n        \n        # --- (Howard Idea 3) Key Number Handling ---\n        mandatory_keys = []\n        if key:\n            mandatory_keys = sorted(list(set(key) & set(active_numbers)))\n            if len(mandatory_keys) > target_pick:\n                raise ValueError(f\"Too many mandatory keys ({len(mandatory_keys)}) for pick-{target_pick}\")\n        \n        n_to_sample = target_pick - len(mandatory_keys)\n\n        tickets: list[list[int]] = []\n        # Max attempts to find harmonious tickets\n        max_attempts = count * 20\n        attempts = 0\n        \n        while len(tickets) < count and attempts < max_attempts:\n            attempts += 1\n            \n            # --- (Howard Idea 3) Key Number Injection ---\n            remaining_normed = {n: v for n, v in normed.items() if n not in mandatory_keys}\n            if n_to_sample > 0:\n                sampled = _weighted_sample(list(remaining_normed.keys()), remaining_normed, n_to_sample, temperature, rng=local_rng)\n                ticket = sorted(list(sampled) + mandatory_keys)\n            else:\n                ticket = sorted(mandatory_keys)\n            \n            # (v9.0) Atmospheric Muon Flux: Simulate a Single-Event Upset (bit flip)\n            # A cosmic ray (muon) has a very small chance (e.g., 1 in 1000) of striking \n            # the simulation memory and flipping a bit, mutating one number.\n            muon_hit = False\n            if local_rng.random() < 0.005:  # 0.5% chance per ticket for fun, realistically much lower\n                muon_hit = True\n                flip_idx = local_rng.randint(0, target_pick - 1)\n                \n                # 'Flip' the number: e.g. bitwise XOR with 1, 2, 4, 8, 16 or 32\n                bit_flip = 1 << local_rng.randint(0, 5) \n                mutated = ticket[flip_idx] ^ bit_flip\n                \n                # Keep it in range and unique\n                if mutated < lo: mutated = lo\n                if mutated > hi: mutated = hi\n                \n                # If collision, just nudge it\n                while mutated in ticket:\n                    mutated = ((mutated - lo + 1) % (hi - lo + 1)) + lo\n                    \n                ticket[flip_idx] = mutated\n                ticket.sort()\n            \n            # If we already have this ticket, skip\n            if ticket in tickets:\n                continue\n                \n            # Filter check: only apply when filters were explicitly requested.\n            # - filters=None  → no structural constraints (pass-through; esoteric strategies\n            #                   produce their raw output, not silently reshaped).\n            # - filters=[]    → same as None; also pass-through.\n            # - filters=[\"balanced\"] → sum_range + parity + breadth (former always-on default).\n            # - filters=[\"all\"] → full constraint set.\n            # For multiple bets (target_pick != rules.pick_count) harmony checks are always skipped.\n            last_draw = list(df.iloc[-1][\"numbers\"]) if not df.empty else []\n            filters_active = bool(filters)\n            if (target_pick != rules.pick_count\n                    or not filters_active\n                    or self.is_harmonious(ticket, rules, filters=filters, k_of_n=filters_k_of_n, last_draw=last_draw, df=df, **kwargs)\n                    or attempts > (max_attempts * 0.8)):\n                if muon_hit:\n                    # Hacky way to inject ticket-specific metadata up\n                    self._last_muon_strike = True\n                tickets.append(ticket)\n\n        confidence = float(np.mean(list(normed.values())))\n\n        # Dynamic parameter capture for transparency\n        import inspect\n        params = {}\n        try:\n            sig = inspect.signature(self.__class__.__init__)\n            for p_name in sig.parameters:\n                if p_name == \"self\":\n                    continue\n                if hasattr(self, p_name):\n                    val = getattr(self, p_name)\n                    params[p_name] = val.isoformat() if hasattr(val, \"isoformat\") else val\n        except Exception:\n            pass\n\n        return SuggestionResult(\n            strategy_name=self.name,\n            tickets=tickets,\n            scores=normed,\n            confidence=confidence,\n            metadata={\"parameters\": params}\n        )\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Weighted sampling helper\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\ndef _weighted_sample(\n    numbers: list[int],\n    scores: dict[int, float],\n    k: int,\n    temperature: float = 1.0,\n    rng: Any = None,\n) -> list[int]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Sample k numbers without replacement using temperature-scaled weights.\n\n    temperature = 0   → top-k (deterministic)\n    temperature = 1   → proportional to score\n    temperature > 1   → flatter distribution (more random)\n    temperature < 1   → sharper distribution (more confident)\n    \"\"\"\n    import numpy as np # Local import for performance\n    if temperature <= 0:\n        return sorted(numbers, key=lambda n: scores.get(n, 0.0), reverse=True)[:k]\n\n    raw = np.array([max(scores.get(n, 1e-9), 1e-9) for n in numbers], dtype=float)\n    raw = raw ** (1.0 / temperature)\n    probs = raw / raw.sum()\n\n    if rng and hasattr(rng, 'random'):\n        # Map python random instance to a local numpy generator for consistent seeding\n        gen = np.random.default_rng(rng.getrandbits(32))\n        chosen = gen.choice(numbers, size=k, replace=False, p=probs)\n    else:\n        chosen = np.random.choice(numbers, size=k, replace=False, p=probs)\n        \n    return chosen.tolist()\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Registry\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n_REGISTRY: dict[str, type[BaseStrategy]] = {}\n\n\ndef register(cls: type[BaseStrategy]) -> type[BaseStrategy]:\n    \"\"\"Class decorator — registers a strategy by its .name attribute.\"\"\"\n    _REGISTRY[cls.name] = cls\n    return cls\n\n\n# --- Registry Helpers ---\n\ndef bootstrap_registry():\n    \"\"\"No longer eagerly imports everything. Kept for backward compatibility.\"\"\"\n    pass\n\ndef _load_strategy(name: str):\n    \"\"\"Dynamically import a strategy module by name using the Registry.\"\"\"\n    if name in _REGISTRY:\n        return\n    \n    config = STRATEGY_REGISTRY.get(name)\n    if config and \"module\" in config:\n        import importlib\n        try:\n            importlib.import_module(config[\"module\"])\n        except Exception as e:\n            import logging\n            logging.debug(f\"Failed to lazy-load strategy {name} from {config['module']}: {e}\")\n\n# --- Strategy Presets 📂 ---\n\n# Curated presets that mix tiers or select specific high-value subsets\nCURATED_PRESETS = {\n    \"chaos\": [\n        \"kabbalistic\",\n        \"reincarnation\",\n        \"lorentz\",\n        \"sentiment\",\n        \"refraction\",\n        \"seismic\"\n    ],\n    \"default\": [\n        \"weighted\",\n        \"markov\",\n        \"bayesian\",\n        \"momentum\",\n        \"streak\",\n        \"kabbalistic\",\n        \"synapse\"\n    ],\n    \"fast\": [\n        \"bayesian\",\n        \"weighted\",\n        \"monte_carlo\"\n    ],\n    \"esoteric\": [\n        \"moon_phase\",\n        \"solar\",\n        \"noosphere\",\n        \"numerology\",\n        \"fibonacci\",\n        \"kabbalistic\"\n    ]\n}\n\n# Automatically generate tier-based presets from the registry\ndef _generate_presets():\n    from collections import defaultdict\n    presets = defaultdict(list)\n    for name, config in STRATEGY_REGISTRY.items():\n        presets[config[\"tier\"]].append(name)\n    \n    # Merge with curated ones\n    presets.update(CURATED_PRESETS)\n    return dict(presets)\n\nSTRATEGY_PRESETS = _generate_presets()\n\ndef get_strategy(name: str, **kwargs: Any) -> BaseStrategy:\n    \"\"\"Instantiate a strategy by name with optional arguments. Raises KeyError if unknown.\"\"\"\n    # Support ensemble syntax: \"voting:markov,weighted\"\n    members = None\n    requested_base = name\n    if \":\" in name and name not in _REGISTRY:\n        requested_base, members_str = name.split(\":\", 1)\n        members = [m.strip() for m in members_str.split(\",\") if m.strip()]\n        \n    # Lazy load the requested strategy\n    if requested_base not in _REGISTRY:\n        _load_strategy(requested_base)\n\n    if requested_base not in _REGISTRY:\n        available = \", \".join(sorted(_REGISTRY))\n        raise KeyError(f\"Unknown strategy {name!r}. Available: {available}\")\n    \n    cls = _REGISTRY[requested_base]\n    # ... rest of get_strategy implementation ...\n    \n    # Handle classes with no custom __init__ (object.__init__ rejects arguments)\n    if cls.__init__ is object.__init__:\n        return cls()\n\n    # Surgical instantiation: only pass kwargs that the strategy's __init__ accepts\n    import inspect\n    sig = inspect.signature(cls.__init__)\n    \n    # Merge members into kwargs if present\n    if members is not None:\n        kwargs[\"members\"] = members\n\n    has_kwargs = any(p.kind == inspect.Parameter.VAR_KEYWORD for p in sig.parameters.values())\n    \n    if has_kwargs:\n        return cls(**kwargs)\n    \n    filtered = {k: v for k, v in kwargs.items() if k in sig.parameters}\n    return cls(**filtered)\n\n\ndef list_strategies() -> list[dict[str, str]]:\n    \"\"\"Return a list of {name, tier, description} dicts for all strategies in the registry.\"\"\"\n    results = []\n    # Use registry for fast reporting without loading classes\n    for name, config in STRATEGY_REGISTRY.items():\n        results.append({\n            \"name\": name,\n            \"tier\": config[\"tier\"],\n            \"description\": config[\"description\"]\n        })\n    \n    # Also include any dynamically registered ones not in the hardcoded registry\n    for name, cls in _REGISTRY.items():\n        if name not in STRATEGY_REGISTRY:\n            results.append({\n                \"name\": name,\n                \"tier\": cls.tier,\n                \"description\": cls.description\n            })\n            \n    return results\nProcess Group PGID: 2481793"
  }

    "output": "Output: \"\"\"\nlottery-engine CLI 🚀\n====================\nMain entry point for the modular CLI.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport logging\nimport sys\nimport os\nfrom typing import Annotated, Optional\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nimport typer\nfrom rich.console import Console, Group\nfrom rich.panel import Panel\nfrom rich.table import Table\nfrom rich.text import Text\nfrom rich.columns import Columns\n\n# --- Command Panel Constants ---\nPANEL_PRIMARY   = \"⭐ Primary Golden Path\"\nPANEL_ANALYTICS = \"🔬 Analytics & Diagnostics\"\nPANEL_BETTING   = \"🎟️ Betting & Tactical Tools\"\nPANEL_MONITOR   = \"📓 Monitoring & Logs\"\nPANEL_SYSTEM    = \"⚙️ System & Optimization\"\nPANEL_VERIFY    = \"✅ Verification & Tools\"\n\n# --- UI Helpers ---\n\ndef _get_header():\n    \"\"\"Shared visual header.\"\"\"\n    return Text.from_markup(\n        \"[yellow on black][bold] 🏛️  LOTTERY ENGINE: THE ULTIMATE GUIDE [/bold][/yellow on black]\\n\"\n        \"[dim]High-precision statistical forecasting and environmental resonance engine. Demystifying complex numbers for the player who takes the game into another dimension. [/dim]\\n\"\n    )\n\ndef _get_strategy_legend(compact: bool = False):\n    \"\"\"Dynamically groups and displays all strategy tiers from the registry.\"\"\"\n    from engine.strategies import list_strategies, bootstrap_registry\n    from collections import defaultdict\n    \n    bootstrap_registry()\n    all_strategies = list_strategies()\n    \n    tiers = defaultdict(list)\n    for s in all_strategies:\n        tiers[s[\"tier\"]].append(s[\"name\"])\n        \n    tier_emojis = {\n        \"statistical\": \"📈\",\n        \"ml\": \"🤖\",\n        \"deep\": \"🧠\",\n        \"fun\": \"✨\",\n        \"chaos\": \"🌪️\",\n        \"esoteric\": \"🔮\",\n        \"financial\": \"💸\",\n        \"default\": \"🎯\"\n    }\n\n    # Sort tiers: Put 'Primary' ones first, then alphabetical\n    priority = [\"statistical\", \"ml\", \"deep\", \"fun\"]\n    sorted_tier_keys = sorted(\n        tiers.keys(), \n        key=lambda k: (priority.index(k) if k in priority else 99, k)\n    )\n\n    if compact:\n        output = [\"\\n[bold underline]🧠 STRATEGY TIERS[/]\"]\n        # Sort to ensure core tiers are always at the top\n        #for tier in [\"statistical\", \"ml\", \"deep\", \"fun\"]:\n        for tier in sorted_tier_keys:\n            if tier not in tiers: continue\n            emoji = tier_emojis.get(tier, \"⚡\")\n            names = \", \".join(sorted(tiers[tier]))\n            output.append(f\" {emoji} [bold]{tier.upper()}:[/] [dim]{names}[/]\")\n        \n        return \"\\n\".join(output)\n\n    # High-fidelity Rich Table for the 'help' command\n    table = Table(box=None, padding=(0, 2), show_header=False, expand=True)\n    table.add_column(\"Tier\", style=\"bold yellow\", width=20)\n    table.add_column(\"Strategies\", style=\"italic dim\")\n\n    for tier in sorted_tier_keys:\n        emoji = tier_emojis.get(tier, \"⚡\")\n        # Use Columns to prevent long lists from breaking the layout\n        strat_names = sorted(tiers[tier])\n        strat_grid = Columns(strat_names, equal=True, expand=True)\n        table.add_row(f\"{emoji} {tier.upper()}\", strat_grid)\n\n    return Panel(table, title=\"[bold white]🧠 STRATEGY ALGORITHMS[/bold white]\", border_style=\"bright_black\", expand=False)\n\ndef _get_help_panel() -> Panel:\n    \"\"\"Generate the high-fidelity structured TUI help panel.\"\"\"\n    from typer.main import get_command\n    click_app = get_command(app)\n    \n    def make_cat_section(title: str, commands: list):\nProcess Group PGID: 2481833"
  }
}

Top Scored Table (Evidence)\n    evidence = None\n    if explain:\n        # Feature 5: score entropy (concentration)\n        from engine.cli.utils import calculate_shannon_entropy\n        ent = calculate_shannon_entropy(result.scores)\n        \n        # Max theoretical entropy for this pool size\n        n_items = len(result.scores)\n        max_ent = np.log(n_items)\n        concentration = 1.0 - (ent / (max_ent or 1.0))\n        \n        conc_label = \"diffuse\" if concentration < 0.1 else \"normal\" if concentration < 0.25 else \"concentrated\"\n        \n        evidence = ui_table(columns=[\"Rank\", \"Number\", \"Score\"])\n        sorted_scores = sorted(result.scores.items(), key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)[:top_scored]\n        for i, (num, score) in enumerate(sorted_scores):\n            evidence.add_row(str(i+1), f\"[bold]{num}[/]\", f\"{score:.4f}\")\n        \n        rprint(f\" [dim]⌞ Score Entropy: {ent:.4f} ({conc_label})[/]\")\n    rprint(ui_panel(\n        Group(\"\\n\".join(content), \"\\n\", ui_panel(evidence, title=\"Statistical Evidence\", style=\"bright_black\") if evidence else \"\"),\n        title=f\"{label} PREDICTION\",\n        subtitle=subtitle,\n        style=Theme.PRIMARY\n    ))\n\n    # Evidence Panel (from v7.0)\n    _print_evidence_panel(result.metadata)\n\n    # Explain Panel: top contributing features\n    if explain:\n        _print_explain_panel(result, rules, df)\n\ndef _print_evidence_panel(metadata: dict):\n    \"\"\"Draw the fancy 'OSINT' evidence panel.\"\"\"\n    from rich.panel import Panel\n    evidence = []\n    \n    if \"phase_name\" in metadata:\n        evidence.append(f\"🌕 Moon phase: {metadata['phase_name']} ({metadata['phase_ratio']*100:.1f}% through cycle) | Distance: {metadata['moon_distance']} | Tide: {metadata['tidal_intensity']}\")\n        \n    if \"kp\" in metadata:\n        evidence.append(f\"☀️ Solar Weather: {metadata['intensity']} (Planetary K-index: {metadata['kp']})\")\n        \n    if \"jitter_score\" in metadata:\n        evidence.append(f\"🧠 Noosphere Jitter: {metadata['noosphere_intensity']} (Jitter Score: {metadata['jitter_score']}) | Regime: {metadata['regime']}\")\n        \n    if \"seismic_resonance\" in metadata:\n        s = metadata[\"seismic_resonance\"]\n        evidence.append(f\"🫨 Seismic Resonance: {'Active' if s['count'] > 0 else 'Stable'} ({s['count']} recent tremors, max mag: {s['max_mag']})\")\n\n    if \"chaos_boost\" in metadata:\n        evidence.append(f\"🌀 Chaos Entropy Boost: +{metadata['chaos_boost']} applied to sampling temperature\")\n\n    if evidence:\n        console.print(Panel(\"\\n\".join(evidence), title=\"Correlation Evidence\", border_style=\"dim\"))\n\n\ndef _print_explain_panel(result, rules, df) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Explain panel: show top-3 contributing features for the prediction.\"\"\"\n    import numpy as np\n    from rich.panel import Panel\n    from scipy import stats\n\n    lines = []\n\n    # Feature 1: top-scoring numbers and their score percentile\n    sorted_scores = sorted(result.scores.items(), key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n    if sorted_scores:\n        top3 = sorted_scores[:3]\n        for rank, (num, score) in enumerate(top3, 1):\n            pct = score / (sorted_scores[0][1] or 1.0) * 100\n            lines.append(f\"Feature {rank}: Number [bold]{num}[/bold]  score={score:.4f} ({pct:.0f}% of max)\")\n\n    # Feature 4: Chi² regime over last 30 draws\n    try:\n        lo, hi = rules.number_range\n        pool_size = hi - lo + 1\n        flat = [n for d in df.tail(30)[\"numbers\"] for n in d]\n        from collections import Counter\n        counts = Counter(flat)\n        observed = np.array([counts.get(n, 0) for n in range(lo, hi + 1)], dtype=float)\n        expected = np.full(pool_size, observed.sum() / pool_size)\n        chi2, p_val = stats.chisquare(observed, expected)\n        regime = \"non-uniform [red](possible structure)[/red]\" if p_val < 0.05 else \"uniform [green](fair / random)[/green]\"\n        lines.append(f\"Feature 4: 30-draw regime — χ²={chi2:.2f}  p={p_val:.4f}  → {regime}\")\n    except Exception:\n        pass\n\n    # Feature 5: score entropy (concentration)\n    from engine.cli.utils import calculate_shannon_entropy\n    ent = calculate_shannon_entropy(result.scores)\n    max_ent = float(np.log(len(result.scores)) if result.scores else 1.0)\n    concentration = 1.0 - ent / (max_ent or 1.0)\n    conc_label = \"focused\" if concentration > 0.3 else \"diffuse\"\n    lines.append(f\"Feature 5: Score entropy={ent:.4f}  concentration={concentration:.2%} ({conc_label})\")\n\n    if lines:\n        console.print(Panel(\"\\n\".join(lines), title=\"🔍 Explain — Top Contributing Features\", border_style=\"blue\"))\n\n\ndef _print_consensus(results: list[Any]) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Prints where multiple strategies agree on numbers (consensus across strategies).\"\"\"\n    # Using TUI Design Language\n    from engine.cli.utils import ui_panel, Theme\n    from collections import Counter\n    \n    counter = Counter()\n    for res in results:\n        for ticket in res.tickets:\n            for n in ticket:\n                counter[n] += 1\n                \n    if not counter: return\n    \n    # 1. Consensus Content\n    lines = []\n    strategy_count = len(results)\n    \n    for num, votes in counter.most_common(15):\n        # We'll just use a simple bar for now as per design language\n        bar = \"█\" * int(votes) + \"░\" * (max(0, strategy_count - votes))\n        lines.append(f\"   [bold yellow]{num:>2}[/]  {bar}  [dim]{votes} votes[/]\")\n        \n    high_conv = [n for n, v in counter.items() if v >= max(2, strategy_count // 2 + 1)]\n    \n    footer = \"\"\n    if high_conv:\n        footer = f\"\\n[bold green]⭐ High Conviction:[/] {sorted(high_conv)}\"\n\n    # 2. Final Render\n    rprint(ui_panel(\n        Group(\"\\n\".join(lines), footer),\n        title=\"CONSENSUS ANALYSIS\",\n        style=Theme.SECONDARY\n    ))\n\n\ndef _print_evidence_panel(metadata: dict):\n    \"\"\"Prints additional environmental context.\"\"\"\n    # (Future: integrate with ui_panel)\n    pass\n\n\ndef _print_learn_section() -> None:\n    \"\"\"Prints educational take-away.\"\"\"\n    console.print()\n    console.rule(\"[bold magenta]LEARN — How to use these results[/bold magenta]\")\n    console.print(\"  • Look for [bold yellow]Convergence[/bold yellow]: Numbers that multiple strategies agree on.\")\n    console.print(\"  • Respect [bold green]Harmony[/bold green]: The engine auto-filters for sum-range and parity balance.\")\n    console.print(\"  • Play [bold cyan]Diversified[/bold cyan]: Use different strategy tiers (Statistical, ML, Esoteric).\")\nProcess Group PGID: 2484509"
  }
}

Supports comma-separated list (e.g. 'weighted,markov') or 'all'.\")] = None,\n    numbers: Annotated[Optional[str], typer.Option(\"--numbers\", \"-n\", help=\"Specific numbers to check against the draw. E.g. '4 12 23 35 47 60'\")] = None,\n    count: Annotated[int, typer.Option(\"--count\", \"-c\", help=\"Number of tickets to generate (if using --strategy)\")] = 1,\n    temperature: Annotated[float, typer.Option(\"--temp\", \"-t\", help=\"Sampling temperature (if using --strategy)\")] = 0.0,\n    limit: Annotated[Optional[int], typer.Option(\"--limit\", \"-L\", help=\"History limit (recency bias): only use the most recent N draws for scoring\")] = None,\n    summary: Annotated[bool, typer.Option(\"--summary/--no-summary\", help=\"Show a summary report at the end when testing multiple draws\")] = True,\n    show_map: Annotated[bool, typer.Option(\"--map\", \"-m\", help=\"Show board heatmap for each target draw\")] = False,\n    config: Annotated[Optional[Path], typer.Option(\"--config\", help=\"Path to a YAML configuration file for batch strategy testing\")] = None,\n) -> None:\n    \"\"\"\n    🔬 Evaluate one or more strategies against one or more historical draws.\n    \n    This command performs \\\"blind\\\" testing: if you choose strategies, it only\n    gives them access to draws that happened BEFORE the one you are testing.\n    Supports multiple strategies (comma-separated), IDs, and ranges.\n    \"\"\"\n    import pandas as pd\n    from engine.strategies import get_strategy, list_strategies\n\n    if draw_id is None and prev is None:\n        raise typer.BadParameter(\"Provide either DRAW_ID(s) or use --prev/-p. Run 'lottery backtest --help' for examples.\")\n\n    # Resolve strategies (validate before fetch)\n    strategy_names = []\n    config_payload = None\n    \n    if config and config.exists():\n        import yaml\n        try:\n            with open(config, \"r\") as f:\n                config_payload = yaml.safe_load(f)\n            if isinstance(config_payload, list):\n                strategy_names = [s[\"name\"] for s in config_payload if \"name\" in s]\n            elif isinstance(config_payload, dict) and \"strategies\" in config_payload:\n                strategy_names = [s[\"name\"] for s in config_payload[\"strategies\"]]\n        except Exception as exc:\n            console.print(f\"[red]Failed to load config: {exc}[/red]\")\n            raise typer.Exit(1)\n\n    if not strategy_names:\n        if strategy:\n            if strategy.lower() == \"all\":\n                strategy_names = [s[\"name\"] for s in list_strategies()]\n            else:\n                strategy_names = [s.strip() for s in strategy.split(\",\")]\n        else:\n            strategy_names = [\"weighted\"]\n\n    # Validate strategy names early\n    for s_name in strategy_names:\n        try:\n            get_strategy(s_name)\n        except KeyError:\n            console.print(f\"[red]✗ Unknown strategy '{s_name}'. Run 'lottery strategies' for available options.[/red]\")\n            raise typer.Exit(1)\n\n    print_command_summary(\"backtest\", lottery, strategy=strategy, count=count, temp=temperature, limit=limit)\n\n    with console.status(f\"[bold green]Loading {lottery} data…\"):\n        adapter = get_adapter(lottery)\n        df = adapter.fetch()\n\n    # Resolve target draws\n    target_rows = []\n    \n    # Mode A: Relative index via --prev\n    if prev is not None:\n        indices = []\n        for part in prev.split(\",\"):\n            if \"..\" in part:\n                start, end = part.split(\"..\")\n                indices.extend(range(int(start), int(end) + 1))\n            elif \"-\" in part:\n                start, end = part.split(\"-\")\n                indices.extend(range(int(start), int(end) + 1))\n            else:\n                indices.append(int(part))\n        for idx in indices:\n            try: target_rows.append(df.iloc[-idx])\n            except IndexError: console.print(f\"[yellow]⚠ Index {idx} out of range.[/yellow]\")\n\n    # Mode B: Explicit draw_id\n    elif draw_id is not None:\n        ids_to_fetch = []\n        for part in draw_id.split(\",\"):\n            if \"..\" in part:\n                start, end = part.split(\"..\")\n                ids_to_fetch.extend(range(int(start), int(end) + 1))\n            elif \"-\" in part and not part.startswith(\"-\"):\n                start, end = part.split(\"-\")\n                ids_to_fetch.extend(range(int(start), int(end) + 1))\n            else:\n                ids_to_fetch.append(part)\n        for val in ids_to_fetch:\n            try:\n                tid = int(val)\n                matches = df[df[\"draw_id\"] == tid]\n                if not matches.empty: target_rows.append(matches.iloc[0])\n            except ValueError:\n                matches = df[df[\"date\"].astype(str).str.contains(str(val))]\n                if not matches.empty: target_rows.append(matches.iloc[0])\n    else:\n        console.print(\"[yellow]⚠ Provide either DRAW_ID(s) or use --prev/-p.[/yellow]\")\n        raise typer.Exit(1)\n\n    if not target_rows:\n        console.print(\"[red]✗ No draws found to test.[/red]\")\n        raise typer.Exit(1)\n\n    # Sort target rows by draw_id ascending\n    target_rows.sort(key=lambda r: int(r[\"draw_id\"]))\n\n    global_stats = []\n\n    for target_row in target_rows:\n        t_id = int(target_row[\"draw_id\"])\n        t_date = target_row[\"date\"]\n        \n        # Handle NaT / None dates\n        if pd.isna(t_date):\n            # Try to infer date from previous draw or keep as None\n            # Strategies must handle None/NaT gracefully now\n            pass\n        elif hasattr(t_date, \"date\"): \n            t_date = t_date.date()\n\n        console.rule(f\"[bold cyan]BACKTEST — {adapter.rules.name} Draw {t_id} ({t_date})[/bold cyan]\")\n        console.print(f\"  [bold]Winning Numbers:[/bold] [green]{escape(str([int(n) for n in sorted(target_row['numbers'])]))}[/green]\\n\")\n\n        # Data available before the target draw\n        train_df = df[df[\"draw_id\"] < t_id].copy()\n        if train_df.empty:\n            console.print(f\"[yellow]⚠ No historical data available before draw {t_id} — skipping.[/yellow]\")\n            continue\n\n        if show_map:\n            # Heatmap implementation... (omitted for brevity in first draft or kept if needed)\n            pass\n\n        draw_stat = {\n            \"draw_id\": t_id,\n            \"winning_numbers\": sorted(target_row[\"numbers\"]),\n            \"manual_hits\": [],\n            \"strategies\": {}\n        }\n\n        # Mode B: Run strategies\n        for s_idx, s_name in enumerate(strategy_names):\n            console.print(f\"\\n[bold]Simulating strategy:[/bold] [cyan]{s_name}[/cyan]\")\n            \n            strat_kwargs = {}\n            # Load params from config if available\n            if config_payload:\n                strats_list = config_payload if isinstance(config_payload, list) else config_payload.get(\"strategies\", [])\n                # Match by index or name\n                s_conf = strats_list[s_idx] if s_idx < len(strats_list) else {}\n                if s_conf.get(\"name\") == s_name:\n                    strat_kwargs.update(s_conf.get(\"params\", {}))\n\n            if s_name in [\"weather\", \"moon_phase\"]: strat_kwargs[\"upcoming_draw_date\"] = t_date\n            elif s_name in [\"numerology\"]: strat_kwargs[\"target_date\"] = t_date\n            elif s_name in [\"biorhythm\", \"zodiac\"]: strat_kwargs[\"draw_date\"] = t_date\n\n            try:\n                strat_obj = get_strategy(s_name, **strat_kwargs)\n                result = strat_obj.suggest(\n                    train_df, \n                    adapter.rules, \n                    count=count, \n                    temperature=temperature,\n                    history_limit=limit\n                )\n            except Exception as exc:\n                console.print(f\"[red]Failed: {exc}[/red]\")\n                continue\n\n            # Analysis...\n            strat_hits = []\n            for ticket in result.tickets:\n                matches = len(set(ticket) & set(target_row[\"numbers\"]))\n                strat_hits.append(matches)\n\n            sorted_scores = sorted(result.scores.items(), key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n            ranks = {num: rank + 1 for rank, (num, score) in enumerate(sorted_scores)}\n            found_in_top = sum(1 for n in target_row[\"numbers\"] if ranks.get(n, 999) <= adapter.rules.pick_count)\n            avg_rank = sum(ranks.get(n, 0) for n in target_row[\"numbers\"]) / len(target_row[\"numbers\"])\n            \n            draw_stat[\"strategies\"][s_name] = {\n                \"hits\": strat_hits,\n                \"avg_rank\": avg_rank,\n                \"top_n\": found_in_top\n            }\n            \n            console.print(f\"  [dim]Result:[/dim] Avg Rank: [bold]{avg_rank:.1f}[/bold] | Top-{adapter.rules.pick_count} Hits: [bold]{found_in_top}[/bold] | Ticket Hits: [bold]{strat_hits}[/bold]\")\n\n        global_stats.append(draw_stat)\n\n    # --- Global Summary ---\n    if summary and global_stats:\n        min_win = min(adapter.rules.odds.keys()) if adapter.rules.odds else 4\n        summary_table = Table(title=\"BACKTEST COMPARISON SUMMARY\", header_style=\"bold cyan\")\n        summary_table.add_column(\"Strategy\", style=\"cyan\")\n        summary_table.add_column(\"Avg Rank\", justify=\"right\")\n        summary_table.add_column(\"Top-N Capture\", justify=\"right\")\n        summary_table.add_column(\"Best Hit\", justify=\"right\")\n        summary_table.add_column(f\"Win Rate (min {min_win})\", justify=\"right\")\n\n        for s_name in strategy_names:\n            all_ranks = []\n            total_top_n = 0\n            best_hit = 0\n            wins = 0\n            total_tickets = 0\n            \n            for d in global_stats:\n                if s_name in d[\"strategies\"]:\n                    data = d[\"strategies\"][s_name]\n                    all_ranks.append(data[\"avg_rank\"])\n                    total_top_n += data[\"top_n\"]\n                    best_hit = max(best_hit, max(data[\"hits\"]) if data[\"hits\"] else 0)\n                    wins += sum(1 for h in data[\"hits\"] if h >= min_win)\n                    total_tickets += len(data[\"hits\"])\n            \n            if all_ranks:\n                avg_r = sum(all_ranks) / len(all_ranks)\n                cap_pct = (total_top_n / (len(all_ranks) * adapter.rules.pick_count)) * 100\n                win_rate = (wins / total_tickets * 100) if total_tickets > 0 else 0\n                \n                summary_table.add_row(\n                    s_name, f\"{avg_r:.1f}\", f\"{total_top_n} ({cap_pct:.1f}%)\", str(best_hit), f\"{win_rate:.1f}%\"\n                )\n        console.print(summary_table)\nProcess Group PGID: 2484684"

os.environ[\"LOTTERY_STORAGE_ENGINE\"] = storage_engine.lower()\n    \n    if detailed_help:\n        _print_detailed_help(app)\n        raise typer.Exit(0)\n    \n    if verbose:\n        level = logging.INFO\n    else:\n        level = getattr(logging, log_level.upper(), logging.WARNING)\n        \n    logging.getLogger().setLevel(level)\n    \n    if quiet:\n        sys.stderr = open(os.devnull, 'w')\n\ndef _print_detailed_help(typer_app: typer.Typer):\n    \"\"\"Iterate through all commands and print a full manual.\"\"\"\n    from typer.main import get_command\n    import click\n\n    console = Console()\n    click_app = get_command(typer_app)\n    \n    console.print(Panel.fit(\n        \"[bold cyan]Lottery Engine — Detailed Manual[/bold cyan]\\n\"\n        \"[dim]Full listing of commands, parameters, and usage examples.[/dim]\",\n        border_style=\"cyan\"\n    ))\n\n    sorted_commands = sorted(click_app.commands.items())\n\n    for name, cmd in sorted_commands:\n        if cmd.hidden: continue\n        console.rule(f\"[bold green]lottery {name}[/bold green]\", align=\"left\")\n        desc = cmd.help or \"No description available.\"\n        console.print(f\"\\n[italic]{desc.strip()}[/italic]\\n\")\n\n        if cmd.params:\n            param_table = Table(box=None, padding=(0, 2), header_style=\"bold dim\")\n            param_table.add_column(\"Parameter\")\n            param_table.add_column(\"Type\")\n            param_table.add_column(\"Required\")\n            param_table.add_column(\"Description\")\n\n            for param in cmd.params:\n                p_type = param.type.name\n                if isinstance(param.type, click.types.Choice):\n                    p_type = f\"choice({', '.join(param.type.choices)})\"\n                is_required = \"[red]Yes[/red]\" if param.required else \"[dim]No[/dim]\"\n                p_name = f\"--{param.name.replace('_', '-')}\" if isinstance(param, click.Option) else param.name\n                param_table.add_row(f\"[cyan]{p_name}[/cyan]\", p_type, is_required, param.help or \"\")\n            console.print(param_table)\n\n        if cmd.help and (\"Example\" in cmd.help or \"Usage\" in cmd.help):\n            console.print(\"\\n[bold dim]Examples:[/bold dim]\")\n            for line in cmd.help.split(\"\\n\"):\n                if any(x in line for x in [\"Example\", \"Usage\"]) or (line.strip() and line.startswith(\"  \")):\n                    console.print(f\"  [yellow]{line.strip()}[/yellow]\")\n        console.print(\"\")\n\n    console.rule(style=\"cyan\")\n    console.print(\"[dim]Use [bold]--help[/bold] for simplified summary help.[/dim]\\n\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    app()\n"
  }

    "output": "Output: \"\"\"\nWonder Grid Strategy 🔮\n=====================\nBased on Ion Saliu's \"Lotto Wonder Grid\" methodology.\n\nThe strategy works in two phases:\n1. Identify a \"Key/Favorite\" number (the hottest or most overdue).\n2. Pair that Key number with the Top 25% of numbers it historically \n   appears with (high-affinity partners).\n\nThis strategy focuses on affinity and co-occurrence rather than independent \nprobabilities.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom collections import Counter\nfrom itertools import combinations\nfrom typing import Any\n\nimport numpy as np\nimport pandas as pd\n\nfrom engine.adapters import DrawRules\nfrom engine.strategies import BaseStrategy, register\n\n\n@register\nclass WonderGridStrategy(BaseStrategy):\n    name = \"wonder_grid\"\n    description = \"🔮 Ion Saliu's Wonder Grid: Key number paired with top 25% affinity partners\"\n    tier = \"statistical\"\n\n    requires_history = 50\n\n    def __init__(\n        self,\n        key_mode: str = \"hot\",  # \"hot\" | \"overdue\"\n        affinity_pct: float = 0.25,\n    ) -> None:\n        \"\"\"\n        Parameters\n        ----------\n        key_mode\n            How to select the 'Key' number. \n            'hot' selects the number with the highest frequency.\n            'overdue' selects the number with the largest current gap.\n        affinity_pct\n            The percentage of the pool to consider as 'Top Pairs' (default 25%).\n        \"\"\"\n        self.key_mode = key_mode\n        self.affinity_pct = float(affinity_pct)\n\n    def score(self, df: pd.DataFrame, rules: DrawRules, **kwargs: Any) -> dict[int, float]:\n        lo, hi = rules.number_range\n        pick = rules.pick_count\n        pool_size = hi - lo + 1\n        n_draws = len(df)\n        \n        # ── 1. Find the Key Number ───────────────────────────────────────────\n        draws_matrix = np.array([sorted(row) for row in df[\"numbers\"]])\n        flat_draws = draws_matrix.flatten()\n        \n        if self.key_mode == \"hot\":\n            # Most frequent\n            counts = Counter(flat_draws)\n            key_num = counts.most_common(1)[0][0]\n        else:\n            # Most overdue (largest gap)\n            last_indices = np.full(hi + 1, -1)\n            for i, row in enumerate(draws_matrix):\n                last_indices[row] = i\n            gaps = (n_draws - 1) - last_indices[lo:hi+1]\n            key_num = lo + np.argmax(gaps)\n\n        # ── 2. Calculate Affinity (Pairs with Key) ───────────────────────────\n        # Filter draws containing the key number\n        key_mask = [key_num in draw for draw in df[\"numbers\"]]\n        key_draws = df[key_mask]\n        \n        if key_draws.empty:\n            # Fallback if key never appeared (unlikely)\n            return {n: 1.0 / pool_size for n in range(lo, hi + 1)}\n\n        partner_counts: Counter[int] = Counter()\n        for draw in key_draws[\"numbers\"]:\n            for n in draw:\n                if n != key_num:\n                    partner_counts[n] += 1\n        \n        # ── 3. Assign Scores ─────────────────────────────────────────────────\n        # Number of partners to include in the 'Top Affinity' set\n        top_n_partners = max(pick - 1, int(pool_size * self.affinity_pct))\n        \n        top_partners = [n for n, c in partner_counts.most_common(top_n_partners)]\n        top_partner_set = set(top_partners)\n\n        scores = {}\n        for n in range(lo, hi + 1):\n            if n == key_num:\n                # Key number gets the absolute highest priority\n                scores[n] = 1.0\n            elif n in top_partner_set:\n                # Affinity partners get high score\n                # We can also weight them by their relative frequency with the key\n                max_c = partner_counts.most_common(1)[0][1] if partner_counts else 1\n                scores[n] = 0.8 * (partner_counts[n] / max_c) + 0.1\n            else:\n                # The rest get very low score\n                scores[n] = 0.05\n\n        return scores\nProcess Group PGID: 2485544"
  }
}
```

\"tier\": \"deep\", \"description\": \"Recurrent Neural Network (LSTM/GRU)\"},\n    \"cnn_1d\": {\"module\": \"engine.strategies.deep.cnn_1d\", \"tier\": \"deep\", \"description\": \"1D Convolutional Neural Network\"},\n    \n    # Fun / Chaos\n    \"numerology\": {\"module\": \"engine.strategies.fun.numerology\", \"tier\": \"fun\", \"description\": \"Pythagorean Numerology\"},\n    \"moon_phase\": {\"module\": \"engine.strategies.fun.moon_phase\", \"tier\": \"fun\", \"description\": \"Lunar Phase Correlation\"},\n    \"weather\": {\"module\": \"engine.strategies.fun.weather\", \"tier\": \"fun\", \"description\": \"Local Weather Resonance\"},\n    \"biorhythm\": {\"module\": \"engine.strategies.fun.biorhythm\", \"tier\": \"fun\", \"description\": \"Personal Biorhythm Alignment\"},\n    \"fibonacci\": {\"module\": \"engine.strategies.fun.fibonacci\", \"tier\": \"fun\", \"description\": \"Golden Ratio (Phi) intervals\"},\n    \"zodiac\": {\"module\": \"engine.strategies.fun.zodiac\", \"tier\": \"fun\", \"description\": \"Astrological transit alignment\"},\n    \"kabbalistic\": {\"module\": \"engine.strategies.fun.kabbalistic\", \"tier\": \"fun\", \"description\": \"Gematria & Kabbalistic Arcanos\"},\n    \"reincarnation\": {\"module\": \"engine.strategies.fun.reincarnation\", \"tier\": \"fun\", \"description\": \"Past-draw reincarnation\"},\n    \"lorentz\": {\"module\": \"engine.strategies.fun.lorentz\", \"tier\": \"fun\", \"description\": \"Chaotic Attractor Jitter\"},\n    \"sentiment\": {\"module\": \"engine.strategies.fun.sentiment\", \"tier\": \"fun\", \"description\": \"Global Sentiment Flux\"},\n    \"refraction\": {\"module\": \"engine.strategies.fun.refraction\", \"tier\": \"fun\", \"description\": \"Numerical Refraction\"},\n    \"seismic\": {\"module\": \"engine.strategies.fun.seismic\", \"tier\": \"fun\", \"description\": \"Tectonic Plate Resonance\"},\n    \"solar\": {\"module\": \"engine.strategies.fun.solar\", \"tier\": \"fun\", \"description\": \"Geomagnetic K-Index Flux\"},\n    \"noosphere\": {\"module\": \"engine.strategies.fun.noosphere\", \"tier\": \"fun\", \"description\": \"Global Consciousness Jitter\"},\n}\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Result type\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass SuggestionResult:\n    \"\"\"Returned by every strategy's suggest() method.\"\"\"\n\n    strategy_name: str\n    tickets: list[list[int]]        # each ticket is a sorted list of numbers\n    scores: dict[int, float]        # per-number score 0–1\n    confidence: float = 0.0         # strategy's self-reported confidence\n    metadata: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)\n\n    def __repr__(self) -> str:\n        return (\n            f\"SuggestionResult(strategy={self.strategy_name!r}, \"\n            f\"tickets={len(self.tickets)}, confidence={self.confidence:.3f})\"\n        )\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Base class\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\nclass BaseStrategy(ABC):\n    \"\"\"\n    Abstract base for all prediction strategies.\n\n    Subclasses implement score(); suggest() is provided for free.\n\n    Class-level attributes\n    ----------------------\n    name                 : str   — short identifier used in the CLI, e.g. \"markov\".\n    description          : str   — one-line description shown in ``lottery strategies``.\n    requires_history     : int   — minimum number of draws needed before the strategy\n                                   is meaningful (default: 30).\n    tier                 : str   — one of \"statistical\" | \"ml\" | \"deep\" | \"fun\".\n    recent_matches_count : int   — how many recent matched draws to include in the\n                                   ``recent_matches`` metadata list returned by\n                                   correlation strategies (moon_phase, weather).\n                                   Override per strategy or pass ``--recent-matches``\n                                   via the CLI (default: 5).\n    top_numbers_count    : int   — how many top-scoring numbers to include in the\n                                   ``top_numbers`` metadata list returned by\n                                   correlation strategies.  Also drives the display\n                                   in ``lottery suggest``.  Pass ``--top-numbers``\n                                   via the CLI to override (default: 10).\n    \"\"\"\n\n    name: str                        # short identifier used in CLI  e.g. \"markov\"\n    description: str                 # one-line description for lottery list\n    requires_history:     int = 30   # minimum draws needed before strategy is meaningful\n    tier:                 str = \"statistical\"   # \"statistical\" | \"ml\" | \"deep\" | \"fun\"\n    recent_matches_count: int = 5    # rows surfaced in metadata[\"recent_matches\"]\n    top_numbers_count:    int = 10   # rows surfaced in metadata[\"top_numbers\"]\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    # Must override\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    @abstractmethod\n    def score(self, df: pd.DataFrame, rules: DrawRules, **kwargs: Any) -> dict[int, float]:\n        \"\"\"\n        Assign a score 0–1 to every number in the lottery pool.\n        Higher score → the strategy thinks this number is more likely to appear.\n\n        Parameters\n        ----------\n        df    : canonical draw DataFrame (sorted ascending by draw_id)\n        rules : DrawRules for the target lottery\n\n        Returns\n        -------\n        dict mapping every number in range(lo, hi+1) to a float in [0, 1].\n        Scores need not sum to 1 — normalisation happens in suggest().\n        \"\"\"\n        ...\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    # Provided\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    def is_harmonious(self, ticket: list[int], rules: DrawRules, filters: list[str] | None = None, k_of_n: int | None = None, **kwargs: Any) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"\n        Structural Harmony Check with Meta-Logic (K-of-N Fault Tolerance).\n        \"\"\"\n        n_picked = len(ticket)\n        lo, hi = rules.number_range\n        expected_avg = (lo + hi) / 2\n        \n        active_list: list[str]\n        if filters is None:\n            active_list = [\"sum_range\", \"parity\", \"breadth\"]\n        elif \"balanced\" in filters:\n            expanded = [f for f in filters if f != \"balanced\"]\n            expanded += [\"sum_range\", \"parity\", \"breadth\"]\n            active_list = expanded\n        else:\n            active_list = filters\n            \n        VALID_FILTERS = {\n            \"sum_range\", \"parity\", \"breadth\", \"no_consecutive\", \n            \"no_arithmetic\", \"birthday_bias\", \"no_calendar_cluster\",\n            \"no_same_last_digit\", \"repeat_rate\", \"parity_balance\",\n            \"high_low_balance\", \"sum_harmonic\", \"prime_count\",\n            \"ac_value\", \"unit_sum\", \"successive_groups\", \"root_sum\",\n            \"hot_cold\", \"no_historical_dupes\", \"no_multiples_cluster\",\n            \"pos_1_low\", \"pos_last_high\", \"mixed_parity_digits\",\n            \"digit_space_123\", \"successive_end_units\", \"density\",\n            \"fibonacci_count\", \"magic_count\", \"multiples_3_count\", \"frame_count\"\n        }\n\n        if \"all\" in active_list:\n            active = VALID_FILTERS\n        else:\n            active = set(active_list)\n            unknown = active - VALID_FILTERS\n            if unknown:\n                import logging\n                logging.warning(f\"Unknown filters requested: {unknown}. These will be ignored.\")\n        \n        results = []\n\n        # Helper to record results for K-of-N\n        def check(cond: bool):\n            results.append(cond)\n\n        # 26. Density Ratio (Frame/Center)\n        if \"density\" in active:\n            density_param = kwargs.get(\"density\")\n            if density_param:\n                try:\n                    f_target, c_target = map(int, density_param.split(\":\"))\n                    from engine.modules.patterns import get_frame_center_logic\n                    cols = 5 if \"lotofacil\" in rules.name.lower() else 10\n                    max_n = rules.number_range[1]\n                    res = get_frame_center_logic(ticket, cols=cols, max_n=max_n)\n                    check(len(res[\"frame\"]) == f_target and len(res[\"center\"]) == c_target)\n                except ValueError:\n                    check(True) # Fallback if unparseable\n\n        # 1. Dynamic Sum Range\n        if \"sum_range\" in active:\n            t_sum = sum(ticket)\n            ideal_sum = expected_avg * n_picked\n            tolerance = 0.3 if n_picked == rules.pick_count else 0.4\n            check(ideal_sum * (1.0 - tolerance) <= t_sum <= ideal_sum * (1.0 + tolerance))\n\n        # 2. Dynamic Parity Check (Odd/Even)\n        if \"parity\" in active:\n            odds = sum(1 for n in ticket if n % 2 != 0)\n            if n_picked >= 10:\n                check(0.2 * n_picked <= odds <= 0.8 * n_picked)\n            else:\n                check(0 < odds < n_picked)\n            \n        # 3. Breadth Check (Decades)\n        if \"breadth\" in active:\n            decades = {n // 10 for n in ticket}\n            min_decades = 3 if n_picked < 10 else 4\n            check(len(decades) >= min_decades)\n\n        # 4. (Howard Idea 7) No consecutive runs (4+)\n        if \"no_consecutive\" in active:\n            sorted_t = sorted(ticket)\n            consecutive = 1\n            max_c = 1\n            for i in range(len(sorted_t) - 1):\n                if sorted_t[i+1] == sorted_t[i] + 1:\n                    consecutive += 1\n                else:\n                    max_c = max(max_c, consecutive)\n                    consecutive = 1\n            max_c = max(max_c, consecutive)\n            check(max_c < 4)\n\n        # 5. (Howard Idea 7) No arithmetic sequences\n        if \"no_arithmetic\" in active and n_picked >= 3:\n            sorted_t = sorted(ticket)\n            is_arith = False\n            for i in range(len(sorted_t) - 2):\n                diff = sorted_t[i+1] - sorted_t[i]\n                if diff > 1: # consecutive already handled\n                    if sorted_t[i+2] == sorted_t[i+1] + diff:\n                        is_arith = True\n                        break\n            check(not is_arith)\n\n        # 6. (Howard Idea 9) Birthday Bias (Calendar Cluster)\n        if \"birthday_bias\" in active or \"no_calendar_cluster\" in active:\n            birthdays = sum(1 for n in ticket if n <= 31)\n            limit = 4 if n_picked <= 6 else int(n_picked * 0.8)\n            check(not (birthdays >= limit and n_picked <= 31))\n\n        # 7. (Howard Idea 6) No same last digit\n        if \"no_same_last_digit\" in active:\n            last_digits = [n % 10 for n in ticket]\n            from collections import Counter\n            counts = Counter(last_digits)\n            threshold = 3 if n_picked <= 10 else 5\n            check(not any(c >= threshold for c in counts.values()))\n\n        # 8. Historical Repeat Rate (from last draw)\n        if \"repeat_rate\" in active and \"last_draw\" in kwargs:\n            last_nums = set(kwargs[\"last_draw\"])\n            repeats = len(set(ticket) & last_nums)\n            max_repeats = max(2, int(rules.pick_count * 0.4))\n            check(repeats <= max_repeats)\n\n        # 9. (Smart Luck) Parity Balance\n        if \"parity_balance\" in active:\n            odds = sum(1 for n in ticket if n % 2 != 0)\n            evens = n_picked - odds\n            limit = 2 if n_picked <= 6 else 3\n            check(abs(odds - evens) <= limit)\n\n        # 10. (Smart Luck) High/Low Balance\n        if \"high_low_balance\" in active:\n            highs = sum(1 for n in ticket if n > expected_avg)\n            lows = n_picked - highs\n            limit = 2 if n_picked <= 6 else 3\n            check(abs(highs - lows) <= limit)\n                \n        # 11. (Smart Luck) Sum Harmonic\n        if \"sum_harmonic\" in active:\n            from engine.modules import sum_range\n            _, _, mean, sigma = sum_range.most_probable_range(rules)\n            t_sum = sum(ticket)\n            check(abs(t_sum - mean) <= sigma)\n\n        # --- SamLotto Power Pack (Advanced Filters) ---\n        from engine.modules import filters as f_mod\n        \n        if \"prime_count\" in active:\n            target = kwargs.get(\"prime_count\")\n            p_count = f_mod.get_prime_count(ticket)\n            if target is not None:\n                check(p_count == target)\n            else:\n                check(1 <= p_count <= (3 if n_picked <= 6 else 7))\n\n        if \"fibonacci_count\" in active:\n            target = kwargs.get(\"fibonacci_count\")\n            if target is not None:\n                from engine.modules.patterns import get_fibonacci_in_set\n                check(len(get_fibonacci_in_set(ticket)) == target)\n\n        if \"magic_count\" in active:\n            target = kwargs.get(\"magic_count\")\n            if target is not None:\n                from engine.modules.patterns import get_magic_in_set\n                check(len(get_magic_in_set(ticket)) == target)\n\n        if \"multiples_3_count\" in active:\n            target = kwargs.get(\"multiples_3_count\")\n            if target is not None:\n                from engine.modules.patterns import get_multiples_3_in_set\n                check(len(get_multiples_3_in_set(ticket)) == target)\n\n        if \"frame_count\" in active:\n            target = kwargs.get(\"frame_count\")\n            if target is not None:\n                from engine.modules.patterns import get_frame_center_logic\n                cols = 5 if \"lotofacil\" in rules.name.lower() else 10\n                max_n = rules.number_range[1]\n                check(len(get_frame_center_logic(ticket, cols=cols, max_n=max_n)[\"frame\"]) == target)\n\n        if \"ac_value\" in active:\n            ac = f_mod.get_ac_value(ticket)\n            check(ac >= (7 if n_picked == 6 else 10))\n\n        if \"unit_sum\" in active:\n            u_sum = f_mod.get_unit_metrics(ticket)[\"sum\"]\n            check((n_picked * 2) <= u_sum <= (n_picked * 7))\n\n        if \"successive_groups\" in active:\n            groups = f_mod.get_successive_metrics(ticket)[\"groups\"]\n            check(groups <= 2 if n_picked <= 6 else True)\n\n        if \"root_sum\" in active:\n            r_sum = f_mod.get_root_sum(ticket)\n            check(r_sum not in (1, 9))\n\n        if \"hot_cold\" in active and \"df\" in kwargs:\n            df_hist = kwargs[\"df\"]\n            flat = [n for d in df_hist.tail(30)[\"numbers\"] for n in d]\n            from collections import Counter\n            counts = Counter(flat)\n            n_pool = rules.number_range[1] - rules.number_range[0] + 1\n            hot_set = set(sorted(counts, key=counts.get, reverse=True)[:n_pool // 4])\n            hot_count = len(set(ticket) & hot_set)\n            check(hot_count >= (2 if n_picked <= 6 else 4))\n\n        if \"no_historical_dupes\" in active and \"df\" in kwargs:\n            df_hist = kwargs[\"df\"]\n            t_set = set(ticket)\n            is_dupe = any(t_set == set(prev) for prev in df_hist[\"numbers\"])\n            check(not is_dupe)\n\n        if \"no_multiples_cluster\" in active:\n            m7 = f_mod.get_multiples_count(ticket, 7)\n            m10 = f_mod.get_multiples_count(ticket, 10)\n            check(m7 < 3 and m10 < 3)\n\n        # 20. Mixed Parity Digits\n        if \"mixed_parity_digits\" in active:\n            p_metrics = f_mod.get_digit_parity_pairs(ticket)\n            # Most random tickets have a mix. Avoid tickets with 0 mixed parity numbers.\n            check(p_metrics[\"mixed\"] >= 1)\n\n        # 21. Restricted Digit Space (1-2-3)\n        if \"digit_space_123\" in active:\n            d_count = f_mod.get_restricted_digit_space_count(ticket, {0, 1, 2, 3})\n            # Avoid tickets where all numbers are from this restricted space (unlikely)\n            check(d_count < n_picked)\n\n        # 22. Successive End Units (Modular Succession)\n        if \"successive_end_units\" in active:\n            s_end = f_mod.get_successive_end_units(ticket)\n            # Avoid long diagonal patterns (4+)\n            check(s_end < 4)\n\n        # --- POSITIONAL PHYSICS (Ordered Vector Distribution) ---\n        sorted_t = sorted(ticket)\n        if \"pos_1_low\" in active:\n            check(sorted_t[0] <= (hi - lo) * 0.25 + lo)\n        \n        if \"pos_last_high\" in active:\n            check(sorted_t[-1] >= hi - (hi - lo) * 0.25)\n\n        # Meta-Logic Evaluation\n        if not results: return True\n        if k_of_n:\n            return sum(1 for r in results if r) >= k_of_n\n        else:\n            return all(results)\n\n    def suggest(\n        self,\n        df: pd.DataFrame,\n        rules: DrawRules,\n        count: int = 1,\n        temperature: float = 1.0,\n        history_limit: int | None = None,\n        seed: int | None = None,\n        pick: int | None = None,\n        pool: list[int] | None = None,\n        key: list[int] | None = None,\n        filters: list[str] | None = None,\n        filters_k_of_n: int | None = None,\n        **kwargs: Any,\n    ) -> SuggestionResult:\n        \"\"\"\n        Generate `count` ticket suggestions using temperature-based sampling.\n\n        Parameters\n        ----------\n        count          : number of distinct tickets to generate\n        temperature    : sampling sharpness (0=deterministic, 1=proportional, >1=noisy)\n        history_limit  : if set, only use the most recent N draws for scoring\n        seed           : optional random seed for intentional/personalized synchronicity\n        pick           : optional pick count override (for multiple bets)\n        pool           : restrict possible numbers to this subset\n        key            : mandatory numbers to include in every ticket\n        filters        : list of structural filters to apply\n        filters_k_of_n : pass if ticket satisfies at least K of the active filters\n        kwargs         : strategy-specific parameters\n        \"\"\"\n        import numpy as np # Local import for performance\n        # Default to standard pick count from rules if not overridden\n        target_pick = pick if pick is not None else rules.pick_count\n\n        # 0. Adaptive Window Sweep (v8.0)\n        final_history_limit = history_limit\n        adaptive = getattr(self, \"adaptive_window\", False)\n        if adaptive and len(df) > 50:\n            best_limit = None\n            best_precision = -1.0\n            # Test 3 regimes: Fast (20), Medium (50), Deep (All)\n            for test_limit in [20, 50, len(df)]:\n                best_limit = test_limit\n            final_history_limit = best_limit\n\n        # Apply recency bias limit if requested\n        if final_history_limit is not None and final_history_limit > 0:\n            df = df.tail(final_history_limit)\n\n        if len(df) < self.requires_history:\n            raise ValueError(\n                f\"{self.name} requires >= {self.requires_history} draws, \"\n                f\"got {len(df)}.\"\n            )\n            \n        # Warning if running close to the data floor\n        if self.requires_history > 0 and len(df) < (self.requires_history * 1.2):\n            import logging\n            logging.warning(\n                f\"Strategy '{self.name}' is running near its minimum data floor \"\n                f\"({len(df)}/{self.requires_history} draws). Results may be volatile.\"\n            )\n\n        # Only pass kwargs that belong to score\n        score_kwargs = {k: v for k, v in kwargs.items() if k not in [\"filters\", \"k_of_n\", \"density\", \"pool\", \"key\"]}\n        scores = self.score(df, rules, **score_kwargs)\n\n        # Normalise to [0, 1]\n        lo, hi = rules.number_range\n        \n        # --- (Howard Idea 2) Pool Constraint ---\n        if pool:\n            active_numbers = sorted(list(set(pool) & set(range(lo, hi + 1))))\n            if not active_numbers:\n                raise ValueError(\"Provided --pool has no valid numbers for this lottery.\")\n        else:\n            active_numbers = list(range(lo, hi + 1))\n\n        max_s = max(scores.values()) if scores else 1.0\n        min_s = min(scores.values()) if scores else 0.0\n        rng = max_s - min_s or 1.0\n        \n        # Only normalise numbers that are in our active pool\n        normed = {n: (scores.get(n, 0.0) - min_s) / rng for n in active_numbers}\n\n        # 4. Geometric Singularity (v7.0)\n        # Identify top numbers and boost their 'Dodecahedron Neighbors'\n        from engine.modules.geometry import get_dodecahedron_neighbors\n        top_ns = sorted(normed.keys(), key=normed.get, reverse=True)[:3]\n        for top_n in top_ns:\n            neighbors = get_dodecahedron_neighbors(top_n, max_n=hi)\n            for nb in neighbors:\n                if nb in normed:\n                    # Boost neighbors by 15% of the top number's score\n                    normed[nb] = min(1.0, normed[nb] + (normed[top_n] * 0.15))\n\n        # Initialize seeded random if provided\n        import random\n        local_rng = random.Random(seed) if seed is not None else random.Random()\n        \n        # --- (Howard Idea 3) Key Number Handling ---\n        mandatory_keys = []\n        if key:\n            mandatory_keys = sorted(list(set(key) & set(active_numbers)))\n            if len(mandatory_keys) > target_pick:\n                raise ValueError(f\"Too many mandatory keys ({len(mandatory_keys)}) for pick-{target_pick}\")\n        \n        n_to_sample = target_pick - len(mandatory_keys)\n\n        tickets: list[list[int]] = []\n        # Max attempts to find harmonious tickets\n        max_attempts = count * 20\n        attempts = 0\n        \n        while len(tickets) < count and attempts < max_attempts:\n            attempts += 1\n            \n            # --- (Howard Idea 3) Key Number Injection ---\n            remaining_normed = {n: v for n, v in normed.items() if n not in mandatory_keys}\n            if n_to_sample > 0:\n                sampled = _weighted_sample(list(remaining_normed.keys()), remaining_normed, n_to_sample, temperature, rng=local_rng)\n                ticket = sorted(list(sampled) + mandatory_keys)\n            else:\n                ticket = sorted(mandatory_keys)\n            \n            # (v9.0) Atmospheric Muon Flux: Simulate a Single-Event Upset (bit flip)\n            # A cosmic ray (muon) has a very small chance (e.g., 1 in 1000) of striking \n            # the simulation memory and flipping a bit, mutating one number.\n            muon_hit = False\n            if local_rng.random() < 0.005:  # 0.5% chance per ticket for fun, realistically much lower\n                muon_hit = True\n                flip_idx = local_rng.randint(0, target_pick - 1)\n                \n                # 'Flip' the number: e.g. bitwise XOR with 1, 2, 4, 8, 16 or 32\n                bit_flip = 1 << local_rng.randint(0, 5) \n                mutated = ticket[flip_idx] ^ bit_flip\n                \n                # Keep it in range and unique\n                if mutated < lo: mutated = lo\n                if mutated > hi: mutated = hi\n                \n                # If collision, just nudge it\n                while mutated in ticket:\n                    mutated = ((mutated - lo + 1) % (hi - lo + 1)) + lo\n                    \n                ticket[flip_idx] = mutated\n                ticket.sort()\n            \n            # If we already have this ticket, skip\n            if ticket in tickets:\n                continue\n                \n            # Filter check: only apply when filters were explicitly requested.\n            # - filters=None  → no structural constraints (pass-through; esoteric strategies\n            #                   produce their raw output, not silently reshaped).\n            # - filters=[]    → same as None; also pass-through.\n            # - filters=[\"balanced\"] → sum_range + parity + breadth (former always-on default).\n            # - filters=[\"all\"] → full constraint set.\n            # For multiple bets (target_pick != rules.pick_count) harmony checks are always skipped.\n            last_draw = list(df.iloc[-1][\"numbers\"]) if not df.empty else []\n            filters_active = bool(filters)\n            if (target_pick != rules.pick_count\n                    or not filters_active\n                    or self.is_harmonious(ticket, rules, filters=filters, k_of_n=filters_k_of_n, last_draw=last_draw, df=df, **kwargs)\n                    or attempts > (max_attempts * 0.8)):\n                if muon_hit:\n                    # Hacky way to inject ticket-specific metadata up\n                    self._last_muon_strike = True\n                tickets.append(ticket)\n\n        confidence = float(np.mean(list(normed.values())))\n\n        # Dynamic parameter capture for transparency\n        import inspect\n        params = {}\n        try:\n            sig = inspect.signature(self.__class__.__init__)\n            for p_name in sig.parameters:\n                if p_name == \"self\":\n                    continue\n                if hasattr(self, p_name):\n                    val = getattr(self, p_name)\n                    params[p_name] = val.isoformat() if hasattr(val, \"isoformat\") else val\n        except Exception:\n            pass\n\n        return SuggestionResult(\n            strategy_name=self.name,\n            tickets=tickets,\n            scores=normed,\n            confidence=confidence,\n            metadata={\"parameters\": params}\n        )\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Weighted sampling helper\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\ndef _weighted_sample(\n    numbers: list[int],\n    scores: dict[int, float],\n    k: int,\n    temperature: float = 1.0,\n    rng: Any = None,\n) -> list[int]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Sample k numbers without replacement using temperature-scaled weights.\n\n    temperature = 0   → top-k (deterministic)\n    temperature = 1   → proportional to score\n    temperature > 1   → flatter distribution (more random)\n    temperature < 1   → sharper distribution (more confident)\n    \"\"\"\n    import numpy as np # Local import for performance\n    if temperature <= 0:\n        return sorted(numbers, key=lambda n: scores.get(n, 0.0), reverse=True)[:k]\n\n    raw = np.array([max(scores.get(n, 1e-9), 1e-9) for n in numbers], dtype=float)\n    raw = raw ** (1.0 / temperature)\n    probs = raw / raw.sum()\n\n    if rng and hasattr(rng, 'random'):\n        # Map python random instance to a local numpy generator for consistent seeding\n        gen = np.random.default_rng(rng.getrandbits(32))\n        chosen = gen.choice(numbers, size=k, replace=False, p=probs)\n    else:\n        chosen = np.random.choice(numbers, size=k, replace=False, p=probs)\n        \n    return chosen.tolist()\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Registry\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n_REGISTRY: dict[str, type[BaseStrategy]] = {}\n\n\ndef register(cls: type[BaseStrategy]) -> type[BaseStrategy]:\n    \"\"\"Class decorator — registers a strategy by its .name attribute.\"\"\"\n    _REGISTRY[cls.name] = cls\n    return cls\n\n\n# --- Registry Helpers ---\n\ndef bootstrap_registry():\n    \"\"\"No longer eagerly imports everything. Kept for backward compatibility.\"\"\"\n    pass\n\ndef _load_strategy(name: str):\n    \"\"\"Dynamically import a strategy module by name using the Registry.\"\"\"\n    if name in _REGISTRY:\n        return\n    \n    config = STRATEGY_REGISTRY.get(name)\n    if config and \"module\" in config:\n        import importlib\n        try:\n            importlib.import_module(config[\"module\"])\n        except Exception as e:\n            import logging\n            logging.debug(f\"Failed to lazy-load strategy {name} from {config['module']}: {e}\")\n\n# --- Strategy Presets 📂 ---\n\n# Curated presets that mix tiers or select specific high-value subsets\nCURATED_PRESETS = {\n    \"chaos\": [\n        \"kabbalistic\",\n        \"reincarnation\",\n        \"lorentz\",\n        \"sentiment\",\n        \"refraction\",\n        \"seismic\"\n    ],\n    \"default\": [\n        \"weighted\",\n        \"markov\",\n        \"bayesian\",\n        \"momentum\",\n        \"streak\",\n        \"kabbalistic\",\n        \"synapse\"\n    ],\n    \"fast\": [\n        \"bayesian\",\n        \"weighted\",\n        \"monte_carlo\"\n    ],\n    \"esoteric\": [\n        \"moon_phase\",\n        \"solar\",\n        \"noosphere\",\n        \"numerology\",\n        \"fibonacci\",\n        \"kabbalistic\"\n    ]\n}\n\n# Automatically generate tier-based presets from the registry\ndef _generate_presets():\n    from collections import defaultdict\n    presets = defaultdict(list)\n    for name, config in STRATEGY_REGISTRY.items():\n        presets[config[\"tier\"]].append(name)\n    \n    # Merge with curated ones\n    presets.update(CURATED_PRESETS)\n    return dict(presets)\n\nSTRATEGY_PRESETS = _generate_presets()\n\ndef get_strategy(name: str, **kwargs: Any) -> BaseStrategy:\n    \"\"\"Instantiate a strategy by name with optional arguments. Raises KeyError if unknown.\"\"\"\n    # Support ensemble syntax: \"voting:markov,weighted\"\n    members = None\n    requested_base = name\n    if \":\" in name and name not in _REGISTRY:\n        requested_base, members_str = name.split(\":\", 1)\n        members = [m.strip() for m in members_str.split(\",\") if m.strip()]\n        \n    # Lazy load the requested strategy\n    if requested_base not in _REGISTRY:\n        _load_strategy(requested_base)\n\n    if requested_base not in _REGISTRY:\n        available = \", \".join(sorted(_REGISTRY))\n        raise KeyError(f\"Unknown strategy {name!r}. Available: {available}\")\n    \n    cls = _REGISTRY[requested_base]\n    # ... rest of get_strategy implementation ...\n    \n    # Handle classes with no custom __init__ (object.__init__ rejects arguments)\n    if cls.__init__ is object.__init__:\n        return cls()\n\n    # Surgical instantiation: only pass kwargs that the strategy's __init__ accepts\n    import inspect\n    sig = inspect.signature(cls.__init__)\n    \n    # Merge members into kwargs if present\n    if members is not None:\n        kwargs[\"members\"] = members\n\n    has_kwargs = any(p.kind == inspect.Parameter.VAR_KEYWORD for p in sig.parameters.values())\n    \n    if has_kwargs:\n        return cls(**kwargs)\n    \n    filtered = {k: v for k, v in kwargs.items() if k in sig.parameters}\n    return cls(**filtered)\n\n\ndef list_strategies() -> list[dict[str, str]]:\n    \"\"\"Return a list of {name, tier, description} dicts for all strategies in the registry.\"\"\"\n    results = []\n    # Use registry for fast reporting without loading classes\n    for name, config in STRATEGY_REGISTRY.items():\n        results.append({\n            \"name\": name,\n            \"tier\": config[\"tier\"],\n            \"description\": config[\"description\"]\n        })\n    \n    # Also include any dynamically registered ones not in the hardcoded registry\n    for name, cls in _REGISTRY.items():\n        if name not in STRATEGY_REGISTRY:\n            results.append({\n                \"name\": name,\n                \"tier\": cls.tier,\n                \"description\": cls.description\n            })\n            \n    return results\nProcess Group PGID: 2488057"
  }
}

0.1,\n        ) -> None:\n            super().__init__()\n            self.embed = _DrawEmbedding(pool_size, d_model)\n            encoder_layer = nn.TransformerEncoderLayer(\n self.transformer = nn.TransformerEncoder(encoder_layer, num_layers=n_layers)\n            self.head = nn.Linear(d_model, pool_size)\n\n        def forward(self, x: \"torch.Tensor\", causal_mask: bool TransformerStrategy(BaseStrategy):\n    name        = \"transformer\"\n    description = \"🤖 Transformer (multi-head attention) — sequence modelling of draw history\"\n    tier        = \"deep\"\n         v = torch.zeros(pool)\n            for n in nums:\n                if n in idx:\n                    v[idx[n]] = 1.0\n            return v\n\n        sequences = [encode(row[\"numbers\"]) for {
  "id": "run_shell_command_1778195046034_0",
